# AERIAL: more of flight, falls and the ground coming up
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== aerial | moment: takeoff
she crouches, springs, and catches the air on the first stroke
the wind of her rising throws grit in the other's face
she lifts off sideways, low and fast, and only climbs once she is clear
each downbeat booms like a sheet shaken out, and the ground drops further
she rises in a slow, heavy spiral, as if the air itself were thick
she claws her way up the first few lengths, then the air takes her and she climbs easy

== aerial | moment: dive
she drops in a long curve that ends exactly where the other is standing
her shadow grows on the ground, faster than the other can step out of it
the wind roars over her and she narrows her eyes against it, holding the line
she folds tight and falls, and the speed builds until the air hums
she feints one way in the fall and comes down on the other
she comes in so steep there is no time to see her until she is there

== aerial | moment: struck_from_above
there is a rush of air and a weight from nowhere, and she is flat before she understands
the blow comes down through the top of her and the ground pushes back up
she staggers, half crouched, the strike still ringing through her whole body
she hears it coming, the hiss of the fall, and has just time to flinch
she buckles under the drop and only her pride keeps her from going all the way down
the force drives the breath out of her in a single cough

== aerial | moment: carried
the ground pulls away and her stomach stays behind with it
she dangles in the grip, every swing of her body tugging at the hold
the air is cold up here, colder with each wingbeat, and she has no way to get down
the grip on her shifts once and her heart stops, then it closes again
she can see the whole place below now, the scuffs and marks of the fight laid out small
she hangs in the talons, kicking, and the talons only bite in harder | has: talons
she feels the arms locked round her tighten with each beat upward | has: arms

== aerial | moment: grounded
the hurt wing locks open, useless, and she drops like a leaf in still air | has: wings
she beats hard with the good wing and only spins herself round | has: wings
she sees the ground swinging up and cannot level out in time
the wing folds under her mid-stroke and she simply stops flying | has: wings
she comes down in a skidding, flailing landing, the bad wing dragging | has: wings
she tries to glide on the hurt wing, but it will not hold its shape and the glide becomes a fall | has: wings

== aerial | moment: knocked
the strike stops her wings mid-beat and she falls out of the sky | has: wings
she is flung back through the air, wings beating wildly to catch herself | has: wings
the hit spins her round and she loses the air, plummeting
her balance in the air goes all at once, and she is falling before she knows it
she drops, one wing still half spread, the other folded against the hurt | has: wings
the blow knocks the wind out of her wings as much as her body, and she sinks | has: wings

== aerial | moment: strain
each stroke of the bad wing comes slow and short, and she drifts sideways | has: wings
she labours to hold her height, losing a little with every beat
the hurt wing flutters weakly, and she can feel it getting worse | has: wings
she grits through each downstroke, fighting the pull of the ground
every wingbeat is a small agony she has to choose to take
she holds the hurt wing stiff, gliding on it instead of beating, and loses height steadily | has: wings

== aerial | moment: lift_fail
the flyer hauls upward, and the weight below digs in and does not budge
the grip strains and slips, and the flyer flaps clear, the lift abandoned
she braces, the wings beat hard above her, and she stays exactly where she is | has: wings
for a moment both of them hang in the struggle, and then the grip tears loose
the attempt ends in a tangle of grit and air, and the ground keeps her

== aerial | moment: falling
she is still turning in the air when the hit comes, and it spins her faster
there is no ground to brace against; the blow simply sends her somewhere else
she takes the strike in mid-fall and lands a heartbeat later, twice hurt
the blow knocks her sideways in the air and the landing comes at a worse angle
the air is no help at all; it only lets her be hit wherever the other likes

== aerial | moment: spiked
she is driven down so fast the ground is there before she has finished falling
the impact punches her into the dirt and drives a long groan out of her
she hits and the ground answers with a dull, heavy thump she feels all the way through her
she is slammed flat, and the flyer is already climbing away before she can move
the force of it pushes her down into the earth and holds her there a moment

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# KNOCKOUT, COLLAPSE, AFTERMATH: more of the end of it
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== knockout
her eyes go blank in the middle of the blow, the lights simply out
she sags, folding at every joint at once, and is down before the sound of the hit has faded
the tension drains from her all in one go, and she drops like cloth
she tips slowly to one side, her face empty, and does not try to stop the fall
her head lolls as she goes down, loose and heavy
the breath leaves her in a small sound, and then there is just the slow rise and fall of her side
she lands without a flinch, without a cry, as if the ground did not hurt at all
her limbs twitch once, then lie loose where they fell
her eyes roll up and close, and the fight is gone out of her face
for a heartbeat she is still upright, swaying, and then all of her lets go
she crumples, not like someone falling but like someone set down
her mouth falls a little open and stays that way, breathing slow
she goes down in a heap and stays there, still breathing, far away
her coils slacken all along their length and spill flat on the ground | has: coils
her ears drop sideways and stay there | has: ears
her feathers settle round her loose and rumpled, no longer held in place | has: feathers
her flame sinks low and steadies, a quiet ember | has: flame
her arms drop to her sides and stay there, palms open | has: arms
her legs give way and fold under her, and she lies where they put her | has: legs
her crest lies down flat and does not lift again | has: crest

== knockout_watch
she waits for the eyes to open, and they do not
she listens to the slow breathing and feels her own heart start to come down
she does not go closer; she does not trust it yet
the stillness in front of her is so complete it rings
her body is still coiled for the next exchange, and there is no next exchange
she lets her guard down a little at a time, watching for the trick that does not come
she looks at what she has done, and the looking is strange
she feels the fight go out of her own limbs as surely as it went out of the other's
the sound of her own panting fills the place
she stands there a long moment, and nothing happens, and she understands
a slow wave of something like relief goes through her, and after it, the tiredness
she is glad, oddly, of every breath she sees the other take
she takes one step back, then another, and only then lets herself breathe
the place seems very quiet now, very wide, and very still
she notices she is shaking, and cannot tell if it is from effort or something else
she half expects the other to rise, and when she does not, she has to look away
she stands guard over the stillness out of habit, and only slowly stops
her own hurts begin to speak up, one by one, now that there is room for them
she lowers her head and breathes, and breathes, and the world comes back
she lets her wings sag open, too tired to fold them | has: wings
her tail drops to the ground behind her, its work done | has: tail

== collapse
her body gives up the effort a little at a time, and then all at once
she goes down slowly, one part after another, until there is nothing left standing
she sways, tries to catch herself, and only finds the ground
her limbs tremble and then simply stop holding
she sinks down as if through deep water, unhurried and unable to stop
she tilts, drops to one side, and lies there blinking up at nothing
there is no pain in it, only a sudden absence of strength
she tells herself to stand and nothing answers
her breath goes out of her in a long sigh as she goes down
she slumps forward and her face meets the ground before she can turn it
the effort of staying up becomes too much, and she lets go of it
her eyes stay open as she falls, wide and surprised
she settles on the ground in a heap, all the tension gone out of her
she makes one more try to rise, and her body only shudders
her head drops, then the rest of her follows it down
her vision tunnels and the ground arrives softly, almost gently
she folds at the middle, then all the way down
her legs splay out from under her and she sits, then sags flat | has: legs
her coils slump and spread, the strength gone out of every length of her | has: coils
her wings droop open and drag in the dirt as she goes down | has: wings
she gets an arm out to catch herself and it folds under her | has: arms
she is too tired even to fall properly; she just sinks | state: spent

== aftermath_winner
she lets her breath go in a long, unsteady rush, and only then realises she was holding it
the tremor starts in her limbs as soon as the need for them ends
she lowers herself carefully, every hurt arguing with the motion
the place is quiet now, and she can hear her own heart slowing
she touches a sore spot, finds it worse than she thought, and lets it be
she cannot stop watching the other, as if the fight might still come back
the heat of the fight drains out of her and leaves her cold and stiff
she finds herself swaying where she stands and widens her stance to stop it
one of her hurts throbs louder now that nothing else is shouting over it
she licks a split lip and tastes the fight on it
she closes her eyes for a moment and the whole place seems to tilt
she feels the ache settle into every part that took a hit, one after another
a strange emptiness follows the fight, where the purpose of it was
she is suddenly very thirsty, and very tired, and very aware of everything that hurts
she straightens slowly, taking stock, and the stock is not good, but she is standing
she lets her guard down a piece at a time, as if unbuckling armour
her ears turn toward the other, still listening for a sign of movement | has: ears
she folds her wings with care, wincing where a stroke went wrong | has: wings
she shakes her feathers loose and they settle crooked, ruffled from the fight | has: feathers
she sits down heavily, not meaning to, her legs simply done | has: legs
she sinks onto her coils and rests her head low, breathing hard | has: coils
she can barely stand; winning has taken nearly everything she had | state: tired, spent
she is still breathing easily, and that surprises her more than winning does | state: fresh, winded

== aftermath_fallen
she lies very still, her breath barely stirring the dust by her mouth
a faint frown stays on her face, as if she were still thinking about the fight
her body has settled into the ground the way water settles into a hollow
the hurt places are plain to see now that she is not hiding them
her side rises and falls, slow and steady and far away
one limb lies at an odd angle, the way it landed, and she has not moved it
the tension has gone out of her face, and she looks smaller for it
dust drifts down onto her and stays where it lands
a small twitch runs along her body and fades
she looks almost peaceful, if not for the marks the fight has left
her mouth is slightly open, breathing slow and shallow
the light falls across her and picks out each bruise
her eyes are closed, and her brow smooths slowly as the moments pass
her breath catches once, then evens out again
a fine shiver passes through her and is gone
she lies on her side with one limb tucked under her, the way she fell
the stillness of her is the loudest thing in the place
her wings lie spread where they fell, one half folded under her | has: wings
her coils are loose and spread out across the ground | has: coils
her feathers are rumpled and still, lifting a little in the air | has: feathers
her tail lies straight out behind her, quiet for the first time | has: tail
her flame burns low and steady, the only thing still awake | has: flame

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# DEVASTATING BLOWS: more thoughts on both sides
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== devastated_thought | state: fresh, winded
*That— how did she find that?*
*Breathe. Just breathe first.*
*Don't let her see. Don't let her see.*
*All right. That one counts.*
*Where's the ground? There. Good.*
*Move. Hurting can wait.*
*I didn't even see it.*
*She'll go there again. Cover it.*
*No. I'm fine. I'm fine.*
*That's going to cost me later.*

== devastated_thought | state: tired, spent
*Too much. That's too much.*
*Everything's ringing.*
*Where am I? Where is she?*
*Hold on. Just hold on.*
*Not that. Anything but that again.*
*It won't stop. Why won't it stop?*
*Stay awake. Stay here.*
*I can't feel the edge of it.*
*Please. Not now.*
*Up. Somehow. Up.*
*I can't do that again.*

== devastating_thought
*That one went all the way through.*
*Exactly there. Exactly.*
*She won't shake that off.*
*Felt it land right in the middle.*
*Couldn't have placed it better.*
*That's the one she'll remember.*
*She didn't even have time to brace.*
*There it is. Everything she had, gone out of her.*
*I felt it go in deep.*
*That's what all the others were for.*
*She went soft under it. She felt it.*
*Never landed one like that before.*
*The whole fight was building to that.*
*Don't stop now. Not after that.*
*Look at her face. She knows.*
*Right on the hurt. Right on it.*
*That was perfect. Don't think. Follow it.*
*She's not going to forget where that came from.*
*All that work, and it paid in one.*
*She bent round it. I saw her bend.*

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# STATUS SEEN: more of the one who notices an opening and goes for it
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
== status_seen | status: flinched, dazed, off_balance, doubled_over, breathless, sputtering
she sees the guard waver and moves before it can come back
she catches the stumble from the corner of her eye and is already closing
the other's attention slips for half a heartbeat, and she is in that half heartbeat
she reads the wobble in the stance and aims right at it
she doesn't think; the gap is there and her body goes into it
*There. Right there.*
*She's open. Go.*
she sees the other gasping and takes the moment while it lasts
she watches the other blink hard, unfocused, and strikes before the focus returns
the lean is too far, the balance gone, and she shoves into it

== status_seen | status: stiff, cramped, bound
she sees the limb answer late and times the next move to the lateness
she notes the stiffness and circles to the slow side
she sees the hold has taken the spring out of the other and presses that advantage
*She can't turn that side. Use it.*
she watches the other try to move freely and fail, and goes where the movement can't follow

== status_seen | status: opening
she drives straight into the opening before it can close
she strikes where the guard used to be
the space is there, and she fills it with everything she has
she moves into the gap like water finding the low place
*Now, before she covers it.*
she doesn't hesitate; the opening is the invitation and she takes it

# IMPACT EXPANSION: more reactions, sensations, pain shows and the physical moments of a fight
# (same categories and condition combinations as story_blocks.txt; a few new sensible combinations)

# REACTIONS: the moment of impact, sized to the blow
== reaction | size: glancing
a quick frown, as if someone had stepped on her shadow
she breathes out sharply and the moment is gone
her skin shivers over the spot the way a pond shivers under a dropped leaf
she does not look at it at all, which says enough
a small click of her teeth, irritation more than anything
her chin comes up a fraction, insulted
the touch skids off her and she lets it
she resettles her weight as if nothing had asked her to move it
a dry sniff, and her eyes stay exactly where they were
it costs her a blink, no more
the faint sting makes her lip twitch, and she smooths it at once
a narrow look that promises to remember it
her crest flicks up and lies straight down again | has: crest
a few feathers lift where it brushed her and settle on their own | has: feathers
her wings give one small rustle against her sides | has: wings
her flame leans away from the blow and stands straight again | has: flame
she tips her beak toward the place, considers it, and lets it go | has: beak
her coils shift one loop over and lie still | has: coils
the rocky points of her mane grind once and are quiet | has: mane
she dusts the spot with the back of one paw, almost a sneer | has: arms
her ruff lifts at the edges and settles | has: ruff
her talons shift their grip on the ground and hold | has: talons

== reaction | size: solid
a grunt that comes from low in her belly
the hit pushes a short "huh" out of her, half a question
she bares her teeth at nothing and shakes her head once
her weight goes onto the far side and stays there a beat
a sharp sniff, and her eyes go hard
she squints as if into wind, mouth tight
the breath she had saved for the next move is gone and she has to fetch another
she twists a little, as though she could turn the sting out of herself
a quick, clipped yelp, more anger than pain
her eyes flick to the place and back, measuring it
her mouth pulls sideways and stays crooked for a moment
she sways back from it and comes forward again on the same breath
a hiss, then a long breath out to finish it
the shock of it hums under her skin for a few heartbeats
she takes it with her head down and her back hunched, like rain
her feathers lift along her back in a ridge and only slowly lie down | has: feathers
her crest jerks flat against her head | has: crest
her beak snaps shut hard, a sound like a lid dropped on a box | has: beak
her flame jumps tall and ragged for a breath | has: flame
she half spreads one wing as if to fend it off, too late | has: wings
a ripple runs down the whole length of her coils and out to the tip | has: coils
her talons clench and scrape short grooves in the ground | has: talons
her mane bristles and the hard points of it clatter together | has: mane
her ears flatten sideways, then turn back toward her opponent | has: ears
her tail jerks straight out behind her and slowly curls back | has: tail

== reaction | size: heavy
the blow wrenches a cough out of her and she nearly bites her tongue on it
she hunches round it, mouth open, eyes squeezed to slits
a loud grunt, and her front end dips toward the ground
she goes stiff from head to foot and only her breath moves
her cry comes out flat and short, as if stamped out of her
she spins half round with the force and has to find her opponent again
a whine slips out on the breath after, quieter and worse
her face crumples for a heartbeat and she makes it hard again
she sucks a breath through her teeth that whistles
the strength goes out of her stance and she puts it back piece by piece
she lurches sideways and her balance chases after her
a bellow of hurt, too loud, that she regrets at once
her eyes go wet and blank, and then focused and furious
the hit drives a long groan out of her that she cannot stop halfway
her head goes down as if ducking a second blow that is not coming
she shakes all over, once, the way she would shake off water
her feathers flare out in a ruffled burst and stay raised | has: feathers
her wings bang open with the shock and she drags them shut | has: wings
a hoarse squawk breaks out of her beak before she can stop it | has: beak
her flame gutters low and climbs back up, unsteady | has: flame
her crest slams flat and quivers | has: crest
her coils jerk tight, then loose, as if they had been stung | has: coils
her talons rake the ground backward as she is shoved | has: talons
her tails fan wide behind her and every tip trembles | has: tails
she clutches at the place with her free hand and her face twists | has: arms

== reaction | size: tremendous
the scream catches somewhere in her chest and comes out as a hoarse bark
her body throws itself away from the blow before her mind has caught up
for a moment her limbs do not know which of them should hold her
a strangled howl, and then the ragged sucking for air after it
she reels, eyes rolling, and the cry follows a heartbeat late
she clenches so hard round the hurt that she shakes like a drawn bow
her mouth gapes and shuts and gapes, like a fish on a bank
a wail that rises, hitches, and rises again
the sound she makes bounces back at her off everything around them
she drops her head nearly to the ground and keens into it
she staggers in a half circle, unable to stop her own momentum
the cry is so big it seems to empty her; she sways in the quiet after it
every muscle she owns jumps at once and then hangs slack for a heartbeat
she shrieks, and the shriek turns into coughing
her face shows it all in a moment: lost, then found, then furious
a howl breaks loose, then splinters into gasping
she makes a sound like a kicked door and then no sound at all for a long moment
the shock throws her head back so hard the cry goes straight up
her legs fold and unfold under her as if they were arguing | has: legs
she hugs herself round the hurt, rocking on the spot | has: arms
her forelegs splay and she stops herself just short of the ground | has: forelegs
she rocks back on her heels from it, mouth wide | has: legs
her beak gapes wide and the shriek that comes out of it is harsh enough to hurt her own ears | has: beak
both wings snap fully open and beat once, wildly, at nothing | has: wings
her flame flares huge and then shrinks to a shaking ember | has: flame
her crest stands bolt upright and trembles | has: crest
her coils whip and lash across the ground in one long convulsion | has: coils
the hard points of her mane rattle as she shudders | has: mane
her tails thrash in every direction and then cling to her | has: tails
her talons clench on nothing and stay clenched, trembling | has: talons

# SENSATIONS: what the pain in a part is like, by how bad it now is
== sensation | level: sore
her {part} stings like a slap from a wet branch, sharp and shallow
a warm tingle spreads over her {part} and cools again
her {part} feels faintly bruised, the way it does after a long day's running
there is a small hot spot on her {part} that she keeps almost noticing
her {part} prickles under the skin as though there were grit in it
her {part} protests on the first movement and is quiet on the second
a dull, sulky ache in her {part} that is easy to step over
her {part} feels stiff, as if it had cooled down too fast
the surface of her {part} burns, and the burn is already thinning
a tender patch on her {part}, nothing worse, as long as nothing touches it twice
her {part} hums faintly, like a plucked string going quiet
her {part} is a little slow, a little warm, a little sorry for itself
the sting in her {part} sharpens when she breathes in and fades as she breathes out
her {part} aches in a narrow line where the blow skidded across it
she can feel her heartbeat in her {part} for a few beats, and then she cannot

== sensation | level: hurting
her {part} aches deep and wide, as if the blow had sunk in and spread
a stubborn heat sits in her {part} and does not cool
her {part} tightens up between movements and has to be coaxed loose again
a knot of hurt in her {part} that tugs on everything around it
her {part} throbs slow and heavy, like a drum heard through a wall
every use of her {part} wakes a sharp complaint and leaves a dull one behind
her {part} feels fat and stiff, swelling into its own skin
the hurt in her {part} is a pressure more than a pain, until she moves it
her {part} feels tender from every side at once
a sour, nagging ache in her {part} that she cannot shake out
her {part} stings when it stretches and aches when it bunches
the ache in her {part} has spread a little further each time she checks on it
her {part} feels slow, as if it were wading through something thick
a hot point in her {part} with a cold, numb ring round it
her {part} jars on every step, a small hard knock each time
she can feel the swelling in her {part} pushing at her with each pulse
her {part} cramps once, as a warning, and lets go
the pain in her {part} is steady now, something she can count on being there

== sensation | level: very painful
her {part} pounds like a fist on a door that will not open
a deep, hot ache in her {part} that flares sharp whenever she forgets about it
her {part} feels packed with heat, the skin straining to hold it all
the pain in her {part} has a centre she could point to and edges that keep spreading
her {part} trembles under even small loads, and the trembling hurts
a twisting ache wrings her {part} and does not let go when she breathes
her {part} sends a bright stab up through her at every jolt
the throb in her {part} is so steady she starts breathing in time with it
her {part} feels scraped inside, every movement rubbing on the sore place
something in her {part} clenches when she reaches, a fist closing round the hurt
the pain in her {part} keeps calling her eyes down to it, again and again
her {part} burns in a long stripe and aches in a wide pool round it
she feels each heartbeat as a hard push outward from inside her {part}
her {part} stiffens between moves, and every restart is a fresh wrench
a sick, heavy ache in her {part} that settles in her stomach too
her {part} is too hot, too tight and too loud to ignore
the hurt in her {part} sharpens to a point whenever she breathes in deep
her {part} feels held in a vice that someone keeps tightening a turn at a time

== sensation | level: excruciating
her {part} blazes so fiercely that her eyes stream from it
the pain in her {part} swallows sound; for a moment she hears nothing at all
her {part} feels clamped in burning jaws that will not open
every twitch of her {part} sets off a burst of agony that rings through all of her
her {part} is a wall of pain she keeps walking into
the hurt in her {part} pulses so hard it shakes the breath in her
her {part} throbs with a heat that seems to pour out into the rest of her
the pain in her {part} is too big to brace against; it goes straight through every brace
her {part} feels hammered flat and still being hammered
she cannot find one quiet place anywhere in her {part}
her {part} jolts with sharp, sickening shocks, each one emptying her lungs
the agony in her {part} comes up into her throat and sits there, choking her
her {part} is so tender that the stir of air from her own movement hurts it
her thoughts keep breaking on the pain in her {part} like waves on a rock
her {part} feels pressed in a hot fist and twisted
the pain in her {part} makes her stomach heave and her sight swim

== sensation | level: devastated
her {part} has gone past answering; it only lies there and hurts
the pain in her {part} has a dull, endless roar to it, like water over a weir
her {part} feels hollowed out and filled again with ache
she has stopped trying to use her {part}; even the thought of it hurts
her {part} is heavy, hot and shaking, and all of it is pain
her {part} sends up a thin cry inside her every time her heart beats
the faintest pressure on her {part} turns her whole body into a flinch
her {part} feels like something heavy strapped to her that hurts as if it were her own
there is no edge to the pain in her {part} any more; it simply is her {part}
her {part} quivers on its own, and every quiver is a fresh hurt
she has forgotten what her {part} felt like before; she knows only this
her {part} burns and freezes by turns, and she cannot bear either
her {part} lies still because it can do nothing else, and the stillness aches
the hurt in her {part} has outlasted her flinching; now she only shakes
her {part} seems to drag the rest of her down toward it, an anchor of pain
her {part} hurts so steadily that a moment of less pain startles her

== sensation | zone: head | level: very painful, excruciating, devastated
the ground swims each time she moves her eyes
a heavy pressure fills her skull, as if it were stuffed with wet sand
light hurts; she squints, and the squinting hurts too
her ears hum with a high thin note that will not stop
her thoughts come slow and in the wrong order
a hot, bruised tightness clamps her face from ear to ear

== sensation | zone: head | level: sore, hurting
a pulse taps at her temple, light and quick
her eyes water a little and her sight stays clear
a dull pressure above her eyes, like a storm coming on
her face feels hot on the side that took it

== sensation | zone: neck | level: sore
her throat tickles and she swallows twice to quiet it
a pull down one side of her neck when she looks round

== sensation | zone: neck | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
every swallow catches halfway, as if on a burr
her neck is stiff as a post, and turning it is a negotiation
her throat burns when she breathes fast, so she tries not to
a deep ache at the base of her skull that tightens when she lowers her head
the air feels rough going down, as if it had grit in it
she tastes copper at the back of her throat and swallows it

== sensation | zone: chest | level: sore
her chest feels thumped, like a drum still humming after the beat
a short sharp twinge at the very top of a deep breath

== sensation | zone: chest | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
each breath pushes against something sore that pushes back
a deep bruise sits in the middle of her chest and aches with every heartbeat
she gets air in shallow pulls, never quite reaching the bottom of her lungs
a shout, a cough, a laugh: any of them would hurt, so she does none of them
her chest feels heavy, as if something were sitting on it
a sharp catch at her side halts each breath in the same place

== sensation | zone: belly | level: sore
her stomach gives one small lurch and settles
a dull ache at her middle, the kind that comes after a hard laugh

== sensation | zone: belly | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her middle feels clenched round a hot stone
a slow sick roll in her belly every time she moves too quickly
the hurt pulls her forward at the waist like a hand on a rope
her belly aches deep inside, under the muscle, where she cannot brace it
she feels queasy and hot, and the ground looks further away than it is
a cramping pull low down that comes and goes with her breath

== sensation | zone: back | level: sore
her back feels stiff in one place, as if after a bad night's sleep
a twinge low in her back when she turns, brief and sour

== sensation | zone: back | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated
her back is one long stiff ache from her neck to her hips
the muscles along her spine clench whenever she reaches forward
a hot spot between her shoulders burns when she twists
straightening up feels like pulling against a rope tied low on her back
every jolt runs up her spine like a knock through a beam
her back feels too short, drawn tight round the bruise

== sensation | zone: arm | level: sore
her {part} feels stiff for the first movement and loosens after
a faint ache in her {part} when she reaches out with it

== sensation | zone: arm | level: hurting, very painful, excruciating, devastated

# TELLS: what an opponent can SEE of her
== tell | state: fresh | posture: standing
her weight sits light and centred, ready to go any direction
she watches with interest rather than worry
now and then she shifts her stance purely to make her opponent shift hers
she holds herself loose, nothing clenched, nothing hidden
her feathers lie smooth and tight against her | has: feathers

== tell | state: tired

# BREATH: how she is breathing, by her condition
== breath | state: fresh
she breathes easily, the air going all the way down
a steady rhythm in and out, unbroken by the last exchange
her nostrils barely move; she breathes like a sleeper
she takes a breath and holds it, wound tight, before the move
her breath is quiet enough that she can hear her opponent's
a quick sniff to read the air, and her breathing settles back

== breath | state: winded
she breathes in gulps between each movement
her breath comes loud through her open mouth
she pulls air in through her teeth, hissing a little
she has started breathing on every step instead of every few
she pants, short and quick, and holds the pant still when she needs to aim
a dry rasp at the end of each breath out
her ribs work visibly, pumping

== breath | state: tired
her breathing is a rough sawing in and out, louder than her steps
she gulps air like water after a long thirst
each breath in lifts her head a little; each breath out drops it
she tries to slow her breathing by force and cannot
she has to stop and breathe before she can answer
there is a faint whine riding on her breath now
she drags each breath down into her as if it were heavy
her breath catches and stutters whenever she tries to hold it

== breath | state: spent
her breath rattles faintly going in and drags out slow
she breathes in short, uneven hitches, out of any rhythm
she sucks at the air with her whole body and it barely helps
each breath is a small effort she has to decide to make
her breathing has become the loudest thing about her
the breath goes in shallow and comes out shaking
she pants with her eyes half closed, saving them
her breathing stops for a heartbeat and starts again with a gasp

== breath | zone: chest
she breathes round the hurt, never quite filling up
each breath in ends in a small wince
her breath comes quick and shallow, like a small bird's
she takes a big breath by mistake and it stops halfway with a grunt
she lets the air out slowly, dreading the moment her ribs reach the bottom

== breath | zone: neck
her breath scrapes in like something dragged over gravel
each breath has a faint, high whistle at the top of it
she breathes through her mouth, carefully, keeping her throat still
air sticks in her throat and she has to swallow before she can breathe again

== breath | zone: belly
she breathes with her shoulders, keeping her belly tight
each breath in pushes on the hurt and she flinches from her own breathing
she grunts on every breath out, the sound squeezed up from her belly
she takes air in small sips, stomach held hard
the breath goes in and stops where her middle starts to object

== breath | zone: back
she breathes carefully, as if each breath might shift something in her back
a deep breath tugs at the bruise along her spine, so she breathes shallow
her breathing hitches whenever her back has to move with it

# HELD_IN: the sound she makes, or keeps back
== held_in | state: fresh, winded
a short grunt that she flattens into a breath

a growl comes instead of a cry; she prefers it
she swallows the yelp and it shows only as a jump in her throat

the cry gets as far as her throat and turns back
a snap of her beak, and the cry stays behind it | has: beak
her flame flares instead of her voice | has: flame

== held_in | state: tired
she manages to keep it to a moan, just
the sound slips out low and breathy, half held
she gets her teeth closed on it a beat too late
a whimper starts and she drags it down into a grunt
a hoarse noise that she keeps from rising

a long hiss leaks out of her, and she lets that much go

== held_in | state: spent
a cry comes out of her with every breath, small and helpless
she groans openly; there is nothing left to hide it with
the sound goes up and breaks, and she does not try to mend it
she whines aloud, and the whine does not stop when the worst of it fades

she moans through her open mouth and her eyes stay shut
a thin shriek scrapes out of her beak | has: beak
she yelps, then whimpers, then is quiet only because the air ran out

== held_in | raw: scream, raw
the scream comes and she cannot shape it; it simply pours out
she screams until her voice cracks and then keeps going on the cracked part
she tries to bite down and the scream comes out through her teeth, ragged
the sound she makes is long and raw and she does not hear herself make it
a scream, a gasp, and a second scream, smaller and hoarser

== held_in | raw: contained
she locks her whole face against it and lets only a breath escape
the sound stays behind her teeth, though her eyes give it away
a hiss, held under control to the very end of it

# TRAVEL: how a hurt spreads from the part that took it to the parts beside it
== travel

the blow reaches her {next} through her {part}, as a push and then a pang
her {next} clenches as the hurt in her {part} reaches for it
she feels the hit spread from her {part} into her {next} like a ripple from a stone
her {part} goes numb for a moment and her {next} feels it all instead

a dull ache creeps from her {part} into her {next} over the next few breaths
her {next} buzzes, as if the blow to her {part} had plucked a string between them
her {next} takes the strain her {part} can no longer take, and starts to ache with it
the pain jumps from her {part} to her {next} quicker than she can follow it
her {part} and her {next} throb together now, one sore stretch of her
a bright sting in her {part} and a slow deep echo in her {next}
it goes in at her {part} and pushes out through her {next}
her {next} cramps in sympathy, a beat after her {part} is hit
the hurt in her {part} pulls on her {next} like a rope drawn taut between them

her {next} answers the blow late, with a hot dull pulse
her {part} jolts and her {next} shudders a heartbeat behind it
a hot line runs from her {part} along into her {next} and fades there
her {next} tightens hard to brace her {part}, and the bracing hurts too
pain spills over from her {part} into her {next}, like a cup filled too full
her {part} rings and her {next} hums with it

# RAW: a blow on a part that was already badly hurt
== raw | raw: contained
she goes perfectly still, and only the shaking of her {part} gives her away

her {part} flinches from it and she holds it in place by will alone, jaw clenched

her face empties of everything for a moment, and then she is watching her opponent again

== raw | raw: cry

a ragged shout of pain, cut off when her teeth meet

her voice breaks on the cry and she clamps down on the rest

== raw | raw: scream
she screams, and the sound goes on longer than the blow did
the scream drags her head back and leaves her shaking where she stands
the {part} takes it and the scream comes up out of her in one long ragged pull
she screams, and when it stops she is bent almost double round the {part}
the scream fills the place around them and she cannot stop it until her breath runs out

== raw | raw: raw
she has no breath to scream with: only a raw, cracked sound, and shaking that will not stop
the {part} burns so badly she cannot think; she curls up and the sound keeps coming
her body flinches away from the {part} again and again, as if it could leave it behind
every breath brings a broken little noise with it, and her eyes are blind with tears
she shakes so hard her teeth chatter, the {part} held against her like something burning

== raw | raw: scream, raw
the touch alone is enough; she screams before she has decided anything
she cannot stop the sound and cannot stop the shaking, and does not try to stop either
the {part} has nothing left in it to bear this, and her voice says so

# GUARDS
== guard | moment: block
the blow bursts against the barrier and spills away in a fan of light
she does not even blink as it strikes; the shield takes all of it
the barrier flickers under the impact and holds
her opponent's move dies a hand's width from her face
the force of the meeting stops dead at the shield and goes nowhere
the shield hums after it, and so does the air around her

== guard | moment: reflect
the move comes back the way it came, faster, and her opponent has no time to be surprised
she takes the force in and gives it back in the same breath
it strikes, and the strike rebounds straight into the one who sent it
she turns the whole weight of the blow round and sends it home
her opponent meets her own blow coming back at her, and it is just as hard as she made it

== guard | moment: catch
she takes it on the limb and feels the shock run up into her teeth
the guard holds, but the limb behind it goes numb to the tip
she braces and catches it high, where she is hardest
the impact drives her guard back against her own body, and that holds too
she catches the blow and leans into it, refusing to give ground
the guard is up just in time; the blow lands on it with a hard, dull thump
her coils take the blow in a thick loop and roll with it | has: coils
she meets it with the bend of her wing, and the wing shudders but holds | has: wings
she catches it on a raised forearm, wincing, and shoves it aside | has: arms

== guard | moment: turn
a twist of her body and the blow slides away along her side
she goes with it, guiding it past, and lets it spend itself on the air
she nudges the attack off its line and it misses by a whisker
the force skates across her guard and on into empty ground
she turns it aside so smoothly that her opponent overbalances
the attack meets her guard at a slant and veers off, harmless

# LANDING, DODGE, GETUP
== landing
she lands on her side and the air is driven out of her in a huff
she comes down hard on her front and her chin hits the ground
she rolls once, twice, and stops sprawled with dust in her eyes
the ground jars her from end to end and her teeth click together
she lands on all fours, but her legs fold under the force and she drops flat | has: forelegs
she lands on her back and lies a moment, staring up, before she can turn over
she skids on the slick ground and fetches up against something hard
she hits feet first, too fast, and the impact drives her down onto her haunches | has: legs
she lands awkwardly, half on top of her own limbs, and has to untangle herself
she tumbles over and over, and the world keeps spinning after she has stopped
she comes down with a thud that she feels in her teeth
she lands in a heap, and the heap takes a moment to become her again
the ground scrapes all along her side as she slides
she tries to turn in the air and only half manages, and the half she missed takes the ground
she lands on her own wings and they splay out under her | has: wings
she hits the ground in a long sprawl of coils, and every loop of it jolts | has: coils
she lands in a burst of dust that hangs round her
she bounces off the ground and lands again a body's length further on
she hits and lies flat, dazed, the ground cool under her cheek
she lands hard enough to jolt a grunt out of her and drive her into the dirt

== dodge
she pivots on the spot and the blow brushes past her back
she drops flat and it whistles over
she skips sideways and lets it go by
she twists her head aside and feels the air of it on her face
she leans back just far enough, and not a whisker more
she sees it coming early and is already moving
she ducks under and comes up on the far side
she slides out of the way like water round a stone
she darts back, then in again before the blow is finished
she sidesteps so close that the miss stirs her fur
she lifts off the ground and it passes beneath her | has: wings
she ripples aside in one smooth curve and it strikes empty ground | has: coils
she hops the sweep and lands lightly | has: legs
she rolls out of its path and comes up facing her opponent
she flinches away just in time and the blow catches only air
she lets her legs fold and the blow passes over her head | has: legs
she sways from the hips and the attack misses by a finger's width
she reads the shift of weight and is gone before the blow is
she spins away, and the spin brings her round ready
she bends out of the way as if she had no stiffness in her at all

== getup
she gets her front half up first and drags the rest after it
she pushes herself up on shaking limbs and stands there swaying
she rises in one ragged lurch and almost goes down again
she lies still for a breath, gathers herself, and comes up all at once
she uses her tail as a prop to lever herself upright | has: tail
she spreads her wings against the ground and pushes herself up on them | has: wings
she gathers her coils under her and rears up | has: coils
she climbs to her feet one limb at a time, watching her opponent the whole way
she rolls over, gets her knees under her, and waits for the dizziness to pass | has: legs
she pushes up on her forelegs and staggers as her hind end follows | has: forelegs
she struggles up, slips, and tries again with her teeth gritted
she gets up slowly, wincing, and stands crooked
she shakes the dust off as she rises, as if that had been the point
she hauls herself upright with a groan she does not bother to hide
she rises to a crouch and stays there, not yet ready to trust standing
she gets up because lying there is worse
she comes up fast, too fast, and the world spins; she waits it out
her first try is all wobble; the second puts her on her feet
she rises and plants herself wide, daring the ground to take her again
she comes up spitting dirt, eyes already on her opponent

# CLASH AND CRASH
== clash
the shock of the meeting runs back up both their bodies at once
for a moment the two forces grind against each other and neither yields
light and grit burst out sideways from the place where they meet
the impact throws them both back a step
she feels her own move falter against the other's and pushes harder
the meeting makes a sound like thunder trapped in a small room
the ground between them is scorched and smoking where the two met
both attacks spend themselves at once and leave only ringing air
the force sprays out in every direction, and both of them flinch from it
she gets the worst of it and is shoved back, heels dragging | has: legs
she gets the better of it, and her move breaks through the last of the other's
the clash leaves her ears ringing and her eyes dazzled
heat, cold, light and dust all fight in the gap between them
they hold the struggle for a long breath, straining, before it breaks
the recoil jars her teeth together
both of them stagger back, blinking, waiting for the dust to fall
the meeting point flares bright and collapses into nothing
everything nearby shakes with the force of it
she feels it in her chest, as if she had been struck and had struck back at once
when the light fades they are both still standing, and both shaken

== crash
she slams into it shoulder first and bounces back, dazed
she cannot stop in time and hits it with her full weight
the miss carries her on and she strikes the obstacle with a dull thud
she tumbles over and fetches up against it in a heap
she swerves at the last moment and scrapes along it instead of hitting it square
she hits it head first and her sight swims
she lies stunned against it for a heartbeat, legs still trying to run | has: legs
her momentum carries her into the hard edge, and she yelps
the obstacle takes all her speed and gives none of it back
she spins off it, off balance, and has to scramble to stay up
she hits, rebounds, and sits down hard
the crash leaves her shaken and very angry
she pushes away from it with a groan, rubbing the sore place against her side
grit and loose splinters shower down round her
she hits it with a thump that echoes, and her opponent sees every bit of it
her own speed is her undoing; she pays for every stride of it
she shakes her head, gets her bearings, and turns to find her opponent
she hits it wings half open, and they fold against it with a jolt | has: wings

# DEVASTATING BLOWS
== devastating | reason: position
she was mid-stride, every limb committed, and the blow found her with nothing to brace
she had turned to guard the other side, and the whole of it went in unopposed
it came as she was rising, still off balance, and drove straight through her
she was caught stretched out and open, all her weight going the wrong way
there was no time even to flinch; the blow simply arrived and took her

== devastating | reason: weak_point
the blow landed dead on the deepest hurt in the {part}, as if it had been aimed by memory
the {part} had been holding on by a thread, and the blow cut it
of all the places it could have landed, it found the one in the {part} that could bear it least
it struck the same spot on the {part} that had already taken the worst, and drove the old hurt deeper
the {part} was already swollen and tender, and the blow went straight into the swelling

== devastating | reason: angle
it caught her square, the force going straight in with nothing turned aside
the timing was perfect: it struck just as her guard dropped
it came from below, at exactly the angle to drive all its force up through her
she never saw the line of it until it was already inside her guard
it landed as she leaned into it, her own weight adding to its force

== devastating | reason: ground
she landed on every sore place at once, as if the ground had marked them out
the fall drove her down onto her worst hurts, one after another
she hit the ground on the side that had already taken the most, and it took more
the ground caught her at the worst angle, and every bruise she had woke at once
there was a hard edge exactly where she came down, and it found the hurt place

== devastating_sell | health: fresh | partlevel: light
she stops, utterly still, as if listening to something inside her
the force of it shakes her from end to end, though nothing seems to give
for a moment the confidence drains out of her face, and then she makes it come back

== devastating_sell | health: fresh | partlevel: hurt, ruined
the {part} buckles under it, and she knows the fight has just changed
she staggers, the {part} trembling, and stares at her opponent with new respect
the cry comes up from somewhere deep in her, and she is still strong enough to be shocked by it

== devastating_sell | health: worn | partlevel: light
she stumbles and has to catch herself, and she knows that should not have happened
the blow takes something out of her that she cannot put back
she looks suddenly tired, as if the fight had aged her in a moment

== devastating_sell | health: worn | partlevel: hurt, ruined
the {part} gives way completely, and she cries out at the shock of it
she fights to keep standing, and the {part} fights her
everything she was holding back about the {part} comes out at once

== devastating_sell | health: spent | partlevel: light
she lurches sideways and barely catches herself, as if a string holding her up had been cut
the blow is nothing much, but she has nothing left to absorb it, and she reels
she wavers, and for a moment it looks as if she might simply fold

== devastating_sell | health: spent | partlevel: hurt, ruined
the {part} takes it, and something in her gives way: her whole body sags
she screams, high and broken, and then has no breath left for anything
her eyes go wide and empty, and the {part} jerks against her

== devastated_reaction | state: fresh, winded
she rocks back on her heels, staring, as if she cannot believe what just happened
a hoarse cry is wrung out of her, and she bites down on the rest
she stumbles back a step, shaking her head as if to clear it
her body jerks rigid for a heartbeat, then she forces herself to breathe
she presses against the pain with all her strength, jaw clenched, eyes wet
she gasps, hunches, and then straightens with visible effort
she holds herself together, but only just
the blow knocks the confidence off her face, and she has to put it back
she snarls through the pain, but there is fear in the snarl now

== devastated_reaction | state: tired, spent
she sinks to the ground and cannot get up at once
she shakes so hard that she can barely stay upright
it is too much; she gives a long, breaking cry and does not stop it
she sways and goes down onto her knees, gasping | has: legs
her eyes go blank for a moment, then fill with pain and fear
she curls round the hurt and lies still, trembling
she can only breathe, short and ragged, and nothing else
a thin keening sound comes out of her and goes on
her whole body sags, and only stubbornness keeps her off the ground

# WATER, JUGGLES, FEINTS
== sputter
water rushes up her nose and she chokes, snorting
she coughs and coughs, spraying water with every breath
she gasps and gets only more water, and the panic shows in her eyes
she gags on the water and has to spit it out before she can breathe
her eyes stream and her nose runs and she cannot stop coughing
she tries to breathe and only sputters
water spills from her mouth as she coughs, and she shakes her head hard to clear it
the coughing fit leaves her bent over and wheezing
she can hear the water gurgling in her own chest
every cough sends a jolt through her ribs
she gulps air between coughs, desperate for it
her breathing is wet and ragged, and every breath brings another cough
she spits, coughs, spits again, eyes watering
she sneezes water out and blinks hard
the water leaves her shaken and wheezing, and she backs off to recover
her feathers are soaked and heavy as she coughs | has: feathers

== juggle | moment: up
the blow lifts her off the ground and she flails at the empty air
her stomach drops as the ground falls away from her
she is spun round in the air, limbs everywhere, and cannot tell up from down
she sails up, helpless, and watches the ground getting further away
she rises, weightless, and has no way to stop herself
for a long moment she hangs there, waiting for the fall to take her
the world turns slowly round her as she goes up
she is thrown upward and the breath goes out of her in a gasp

== juggle | moment: caught
the blow catches her as she falls and smashes her back down
she has no footing, no way to brace, and the strike hits her full
she is struck in mid-air and sent crashing to the ground
the second blow takes her before she lands and adds its force to the fall
she is hammered down out of the air and hits hard
the strike meets her at the top of her flight and drives her straight back down
there is nothing to push against, so the blow takes all of her
she is batted out of the air like a leaf in a gust, and comes down hard

== feint | moment: bit
she lunges to block the obvious blow, and the real one comes in behind it
she flinches at the fake and leaves herself wide open
her eyes follow the feint, and she never sees the strike coming
she braces for a blow from one side and is hit from the other
she twists away from the fake and twists straight into the real thing
the feint pulls her guard down, and the blow lands high
she reacts to the shoulder, and the hit comes from the hip
she falls for it completely, and knows it the moment she does
her whole body turns toward a threat that is not there

== feint | moment: read
she sees the fake for what it is and does not move
she waits out the feint and meets the real blow with her guard up
she shifts her weight just enough to cover both, and lets the fake go by
she catches the tell in her opponent's eyes and stays put
the feint is clever, but she has seen that trick before
she ignores the fake entirely and watches for the real strike
she does not take the bait, and her opponent knows it

# ANTICIPATION
== anticipate
she sees it coming and her body clenches round the hurt place
a whimper escapes her as the blow comes toward her sore side
she tries to twist away, but the hurt makes her slow
her eyes widen as she sees where it is going
she curls in to protect the hurt part, and leaves her other side open
she flinches before it lands, and the flinch is almost as bad as the blow
her breath catches in fear as it comes toward her
she turns her body to shield the hurt part, a fraction too late
everything in her tries to pull the hurt part out of the way
she braces, knowing exactly how much this is going to hurt
a cry starts before the blow even reaches her
she shrinks back, eyes on the blow, the hurt part pressed against her
she turns her face away and waits for it to land
her coils draw tight round the hurt place before the blow arrives | has: coils
she pulls her wing in close, trying to cover the hurt place | has: wings
she lifts a hand to shield the hurt part, trembling | has: arms
she tucks her tail in close, as if that could help | has: tail
her crest flattens as she sees it coming | has: crest

== anticipate_seen
she sees the hurt part pulled in, and goes straight for it
the flinch tells her everything she needs to know
she watches her opponent shrink back, and knows exactly where to strike
she aims for the place her opponent is guarding most carefully
the fear in her opponent's eyes tells her where to hit
she sees the hurt side turned away and goes round to find it
the way her opponent shields that part is a map to it
she feints at the hurt part and watches her opponent flinch
she sees the protection and knows the hurt is still there
the guarding is clumsy, and she finds the gap in it
*That's where it hurts. Right there.*
*She's scared of that side. Good.*
*She can't protect it forever.*
*Look at her flinch. She knows what's coming.*
*Go where she's hiding it.*

# FRENZY AND LAST STAND
== frenzy
she stops counting; the blows keep coming without her
the same spot, again and again, until her opponent's voice changes
her breath goes out on every strike, a grunt with each one
each blow starts before the last has finished landing
the strikes run together into one long drumming
she hits until her own limbs begin to burn, and keeps hitting
there is no aim left in it, only the next blow and the one after
her own panting keeps time with the blows
she hardly sees her opponent now; she sees the place, and the place, and the place
the blows get sloppier and harder at the same time
she drives on long after the first ones would have been enough
her arms ache at the shoulder and still they rise and fall | has: arms
a snarl rides on every strike, getting hoarser
she forgets to breathe and only remembers when she has to stop
the rhythm takes her, and she lets it
blow on blow on blow, each landing on top of the last

== last_stand
she gathers herself in one long breath and spends all of it at once
her whole body shakes with the effort before she even moves
she holds nothing back for later; there is no later in this
she rises off the ground with a groan and throws herself forward
she aims everything she has left at one place and goes
it is clumsy and enormous, the strength of someone who has stopped counting the cost
the hurt parts are forgotten; for one moment all of her works again
a hoarse yell goes out ahead of her like a banner
she drags the last strength up from somewhere underneath the pain
she moves faster than she has in a long time, and it costs her everything
she plants her feet and puts her whole weight into it, past caring what comes after | has: legs
her sight narrows to one target and she goes straight at it
the effort leaves her mouth as a raw shout
she is shaking before it starts and shaking after, and steady only for the moment it takes
everything she has been saving goes into it, and she feels herself empty as it leaves
she comes at her opponent the way a falling tree comes down: slow to start, then all at once

# STATUS TELLS
== status_tell | status: flinched
she jerks back from the blow and her guard comes up a beat too late to matter
her eyes shut tight and her body hunches, waiting for a second blow
she twitches all over, every reflex firing at once

== status_tell | status: dazed
she stares at a spot beside her opponent, then finds the right one
her steps wander a little, as if the ground were moving under her
she shakes her head slowly, blinking, and the blinking does not help

== status_tell | status: off_balance
her legs splay as she tries to catch her weight | has: legs
she tips sideways and has to take a quick extra step to stay up
her balance swings like a loose gate and she grabs after it

== status_tell | status: doubled_over
she bends at the middle and her breath comes out in a groan
she cannot unbend; her body will not let her
she hangs over the hurt, head low, mouth open

== status_tell | status: breathless
she gapes and heaves, but the air will not come
her chest jerks with empty pulls, and she can only wait
the world narrows to the need for one breath

== status_tell | status: sputtering
she coughs and splutters, water running from her mouth and nose
she shakes her head hard, coughing, trying to clear her breath

== status_tell | status: stiff
she moves as if she had rusted, every joint slow to bend
the stiffness makes her clumsy; her first movement is a stagger

== status_tell | status: bound
the held limb is going numb, and the grip feels like a cold weight
she strains against the grip and gets nowhere, the limb growing heavier

== status_tell | status: cramped
a muscle knots and will not let go, and she stops dead in the middle of a move
she tries to stretch the cramp out and it only seizes harder
the cramp pulls the limb in against her and holds it there

== status_tell | status: paralyzed
a jolt runs through her and her limbs lock, quivering
she tries to move and her body answers late and jerky
her muscles twitch on their own, and the twitching will not stop

== status_tell | status: confused
she turns toward the wrong place and has to look again
she blinks, slow and puzzled, as if the fight had changed its rules
she lunges at nothing and catches herself, bewildered

== status_tell | status: constricted
the coils press her ribs in and every breath is a small battle
she cannot move her limbs inside the loops; only her head is free

== status_seen | status: flinched, dazed, off_balance, doubled_over, breathless, sputtering
she reads it in the stiff, hunched shape of her opponent, and moves
she catches the moment the guard drops and is already striking
*There it is.*
she does not need a second look; the opening is plain
she steps in before her opponent can collect herself

== status_seen | status: stiff, cramped, bound
she sees the limb answer late, and works round to that side
she notes the stiffness and makes her opponent turn on it
*That one's not working. Use it.*

== status_seen | status: opening
she drives into the gap before it can close
the blow is already in the air as the guard falls away
she takes the opening without stopping to think about it

== status_seen | status: paralyzed, confused
she sees the strange, jerky way her opponent moves, and goes in
she sees her opponent looking the wrong way and takes the chance
she watches the limbs fail to answer and walks straight in
*She doesn't know where I am. Good.*

# CRUSHED: driven into something and held there
== crushed
the weight leans in on her and leans in again, as if testing what she can bear
she is pressed so flat that her own heartbeat seems to push back against it
the hard thing behind her will not give, and the thing in front of her will not stop
her cheek is ground against whatever is behind her, her head turned sideways
her ribs flex and hold, flex and hold, with every fresh shove
the push goes on so long that she stops expecting it to end
her wings are pinned flat behind her, folded awkwardly against the hard surface | has: wings
her coils are mashed against the surface and cannot find room to move | has: coils
the cold of the charge has frozen her fur to the surface behind her | kind: ice
grit grinds between her back and the stone with every push | kind: rock

== crushed | state: fresh, winded
she gets whatever she can against the weight and shoves, and for a moment it actually gives
she grits her teeth and holds, refusing to be flattened
she keeps her eyes open and fixed on the one doing it, furious
she twists her head to find air and drags it in through her teeth
a growl rumbles up against the pressure, muffled but there
her muscles bunch and lock, and she takes it the way a wall takes a wave

== crushed | state: tired, spent
she stops pushing back and the pressure flattens her further
a weak, wheezing sound comes out each time the weight shifts
her limbs go slack against it and her head lolls
she can only take in a sip of air before the pressure takes it back
her eyes close and she simply endures
she makes a small helpless noise, and the weight keeps on

# CONSTRICTION: held in coils
== constriction
the coils shift and slide, finding each place she is soft and settling there
every time she breathes out, the loops take up the slack
the squeeze is slow and even, never quite more than she can bear and never less
she can feel the long muscles moving in the coils around her, unhurried

== constriction | fade: fighting
she strains against the coils with everything she has and they barely flex
she twists and squirms, hunting for a gap between the loops
she snaps at the nearest loop, and the squeeze answers her
she sucks in a breath while she can and holds it, fighting the squeeze

== constriction | fade: weakening
her pushes against the coils come weaker and further apart
she gasps for each breath and gets less each time
her struggles slow, as if she were wading through deep mud
she feels the strength leaking out of her limbs into the squeeze

== constriction | fade: fading
her eyes half close; the squeeze feels distant, as if it were happening to someone else
she can hardly feel her paws any more
her struggles have shrunk to small, aimless movements
each breath is a tiny sip, and she cannot remember the last full one

== constriction | fade: nearly out
her head sags against the coils and stays there
the world is only the slow squeeze and the slower beat of her own heart
she makes one feeble push against the coils and it goes nowhere
her breath is so faint that the coils hardly notice it

# WEATHER
== weather | weather: rain
the rain plasters her fur flat and runs off her in streams
puddles spread across the ground and every step splashes
the rain is cold, and it drums on her back without stopping
her feathers grow sodden and heavy in the downpour | has: feathers
the rain hisses where it strikes her flame, and steam curls up | has: flame
the rain runs down her face and into her mouth, tasting of earth

== weather | weather: sun
the sun beats down on her back, heavy as a hand
the ground is too hot to stand still on
the heat saps her, and her fur feels like a coat she cannot take off
her flame burns brighter in the heat, eager | has: flame
the light flashes off her opponent and blinds her for a heartbeat
her mouth goes dry in the heat

== weather | weather: hail
hailstones rattle off her back and sting her ears | has: ears
the ground turns white with hail and slippery underfoot
a hailstone strikes her square on the head and she flinches
the hail patters on her feathers and stings through them | has: feathers
the hail hisses as it strikes her flame | has: flame
the cold of the hail seeps into her and stiffens her movements

# MOVEMENT: ways to stage an attack, by the kind of move
== movement | kind: claw
she slashes on the way out, so the cut lands as she retreats
two strokes crossing, one high and one low, the second where the first drove her opponent
the claws hook in at the end of the swing and pull her opponent half a step forward
a swipe at the face to make her blink, and then the real rake across the body
she reaches long, further than she seemed able, and only the tips of the claws catch
she strikes as she lands from a leap, the whole drop behind the stroke
a flurry of short scratches, quick and stinging, to break the other's rhythm

== movement | kind: bite
she darts in, snaps, and is back out of reach before the other has finished flinching
she goes for the limb nearest the ground, where it is hardest to pull away
she clamps down and plants herself, turning the bite into a tug of war
her jaws open wide enough to show everything, a threat before the bite
she catches the edge of the body as she passes and drags her teeth along it
she bites, lets go, and bites again a hand's width further along

== movement | kind: charge
she starts slow, a walk, then a trot, and only at the very end the full rush
she charges at a slant, so the impact spins as well as shoves
she builds speed in a curve and arrives from the side the other is not watching
she leaps the last length and lands on the other with her whole weight
she lowers herself until she is almost skimming the ground, then rises into the impact
she charges through the other's guard, not round it
she comes in shoulder down and keeps driving after the hit, legs pumping | has: legs

== movement | kind: tail
the tail hooks round the far side and pulls rather than strikes
she spins on the spot and the tail comes round twice, the second time harder
a low sweep at the legs, then the tail rising for a blow from above
she braces on her front and lashes the tail out behind her like a kick
she uses the tail to throw grit first, and then the blow
the tip of the tail flicks at the eyes while the thick root of it slams into the body

== movement | kind: beam
she fires in short pulses, each one aimed at the last place she hit
she sweeps the beam upward, forcing the other to rise or be caught
she plants herself low and wide and lets the force pour out of her
the beam narrows to a point as she bears down on one spot
she fires once, wide, to herd the other where she wants her, and then the true shot
the beam leaves a line of churned, smoking ground behind it
she holds her breath and fires until her lungs burn, then lets it go

== movement | kind: punch
she slips the guard with a feint and drives a straight blow into the gap | has: arms
a heavy body blow, the kind that bends the other forward | has: arms
a looping strike over the top, coming down on the head | has: arms
she strikes and steps through in one movement, her weight following the blow
a short jab, then another, then a heavy one behind them | has: arms
she shoves the guard aside with one paw and hits through the space with the other | has: arms

== movement | kind: horn
she drives the horn forward with the whole push of her body behind it | has: horn
she lowers her head to aim and the horn swings round like a scythe | has: horn
she hooks the horn under the other's guard and heaves upward | has: horn
a short stab of the horn, quick in and quick out | has: horn
she slashes the horn across, edge first, a flat cut rather than a thrust | has: horn

== movement | kind: throw
she pivots on one foot and flings the other off her hip | has: legs
she drags her opponent round in a full circle before letting go
she lifts her high and slams her flat on her back
she drops her own weight suddenly, pulling the other down over her and past
she throws her into the nearest hard thing, aimed with care
she hauls her up by the scruff and hurls her away with a twist of the neck

== movement | kind: grab
she grabs and pulls tight, chest to chest, so there is no room to strike back
her paws close round the other's limb and hold fast | has: arms
she pins one limb with her body and another with her grip
she takes the scruff and pulls the head down
her coils loop round twice and tighten slowly, steadily | has: coils
her talons close round a limb and squeeze | has: talons

== movement | kind: wing
she spins and the wing comes round with the force of the whole turn | has: wings
she strikes with the wrist of the wing, hard and close | has: wings
she beats the wing down onto the other from above | has: wings
a double strike, both wings at once, from either side | has: wings
the wing slices across, fast and flat, at shoulder height | has: wings

== movement | kind: beak
she stabs with the beak, quick and precise, at the softest place she can see | has: beak
she pecks so fast the beak blurs | has: beak
she grabs with the beak and twists | has: beak
she draws her head back and drives the beak in hard | has: beak
she hammers with the beak, again and again, at one spot | has: beak

== movement | kind: talon
she drops on her target from above, talons first | has: talons
she rakes with the talons as she passes, a slashing grip | has: talons
she seizes with both feet and beats her wings to drive the grip harder | has: wings
she kicks out with one foot, talons spread | has: talons
she lands on the other and grips, talons digging in | has: talons

== movement | kind: close
she pummels at close range, short sharp blows that leave no room to answer
she drives whatever is nearest into the other: shoulder, head, hip
every blow is short and compact, and lands in the same place
she works the body, close in, blow after blow
she butts with her head when there is no room for anything else

== movement | kind: fire
she breathes fire in a long, rolling stream and sweeps it across
a ball of flame forms in her mouth and she hurls it
she sets the ground alight round the other and closes the ring
the fire spits from her in short bursts, each one scorching
heat ripples off her before the fire comes, warping the air
her flame burns brighter and the fire pours out | has: flame

== movement | kind: ice
she breathes a cone of frost that spreads wide, freezing as it goes
shards of ice form in the air and fly at the target
the cold settles over the other like a blanket, slowing her
she freezes the ground under the other's feet
the ice cracks as it strikes, spreading frost over everything it touches
her breath turns to fog and then to ice

== movement | kind: wind
she whips up a gust that drags at the other's footing
the wind spins round the target, trapping her in a vortex
she beats her wings and sends a blast of air across the ground | has: wings
the wind slams into the other like a shove from a giant hand
she sends the gust low along the ground, sweeping the other's legs

== movement | kind: rock
she hurls a boulder with a heave of her whole body
stones burst up from the ground beneath the other
she sends a hail of sharp stones at the target
a slab of rock tilts up out of the ground and topples toward the other
she slams the ground and the shock sends stones flying
the stones spin as they fly, scattering grit everywhere

== movement
she shifts her footing half a step before every move, so the angle is never the same twice
she lets the other strike first and answers into the open space it leaves
she moves in on the other's breath out, when the body is slackest
she circles until the other has to face into the wind
she keeps her eyes on the other's shoulders, not the face
she presses and presses, and only strikes when the other starts to give ground
she stops short, waits, and goes the moment the other relaxes

# build 150: speed built over passes in the air, and strafing passes (a second, glancing strike on the way past)
== aerial | moment: dive | has: wings
each pass faster than the last, the air tearing past her wings as she comes round again
the speed of the last climb still in her, carried straight down into this one
she doesn't stop to hover: she uses the momentum, banks, and comes back faster
a raking touch on the way past, quick and light, before she is gone again
the second blow is barely a blow at all, just her speed brushing past and leaving a sting
her opponent turns to follow her and is already too slow
the wind of her passing hits a beat after she does
she climbs away before the other can answer, already lining up the next run

# build 154: a fighter on the ground climbs high ground and leaps at a flyer; blows struck back as a flyer passes
== aerial | moment: leap
up the side of it in three scrambling bounds, claws and feet finding every knot and ledge
she reaches the top with the flyer still above her and doesn't stop: she uses the height and jumps
a long gathering crouch on the top, and then everything she has goes into the jump
for a moment she is in the air too, nothing under her, everything aimed at the flyer
the flyer sees her coming a beat late and twists away too slowly
the climb costs her breath she doesn't have, but the jump is all spring
she leaves the top with a kick that makes the whole thing shudder behind her
up there, level with the wings for once, she swings with everything
the ground is a long way down, and she isn't thinking about it yet
she comes down from the height on her feet, and the jolt runs up both legs into her hips
she lands in a crouch that buckles nearly to the ground before it holds
she misses, and for one long moment there is only air under her and the ground coming up
== aerial | moment: counter
she lets the dive come, steps aside at the last instant, and swings as it goes past
the flyer's own speed is in the blow that meets her, more than either of them put there
a quick strike at the wing going by, all timing, no wind-up
she doesn't get out of the way entirely, but she gets something back as it passes
for a heartbeat they are side by side, and then the flyer is gone, carrying the sting with her
they cross so close that each takes something from the other
the answering blow catches her flank as she streaks by, knocking her line crooked for a wingbeat
she turns with the pass, reading it, and the counter is already on its way
